- **xo-improve**: Use this skill when the user asks to improve code AGAINST A SPECIFIC BENCHMARK — make it faster, reduce memory, lower cost, hit a target metric. Runs a subagent-driven iteration loop — each round a subagent proposes an optimization, the benchmark runs (not the test suite), keeps if metric improves without regressing correctness. Default 7 iterations. Not for open-ended refinement (use xo-polish), not for finding bugs (use xo-audit). ([XoAnonXo/xoloop](https://github.com/XoAnonXo/xoloop/tree/HEAD/plugins/xoloop/skills/xo-improve) · ★1)
